{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_b987b9bd72a842ab8761940b","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# Domain Check\n2\t\n3\tAuthoritative domain availability checker powered by [RDAP](https://about.rdap.org/) — the ICANN-mandated successor to WHOIS.\n4\t\n5\t## Features\n6\t\n7\t- **Authoritative** — queries registry RDAP servers directly (Verisign, PIR, Google, etc.)\n8\t- **Web UI** — clean, fast, no-signup interface for humans\n9\t- **REST API** — JSON API for programmatic access\n10\t- **CLI** — check domains from the terminal\n11\t- **Bulk checking** — check up to 50 domains in a single request\n12\t- **Multi-TLD** — check a name across multiple TLDs at once\n13\t- **Self-hostable** — single Go binary or Docker container\n14\t- **Zero tracking** — no analytics, no cookies, no data retention\n15\t\n16\t## Why RDAP over WHOIS?\n17\t\n18\t| | RDAP | WHOIS |\n19\t|---|---|---|\n20\t| Protocol | HTTPS (JSON) | TCP port 43 (plaintext) |\n21\t| Accuracy | Definitive | Unreliable (missed registered domains in testing) |\n22\t| .dev/.app support | Yes | No (Google disabled port 43) |\n23\t| Response format | Structured JSON | Unstructured text (varies by registry) |\n24\t| Parsing | Trivial | Fragile regex per-registry |\n25\t\n26\tIn testing, `whois` reported a registered domain as available. RDAP was 100% accurate across all test cases. See [docs/research/05-accuracy-comparison.md](docs/research/05-accuracy-comparison.md).\n27\t\n28\t## Quick Start\n29\t\n30\t### Binary (Linux/macOS)\n31\t\n32\tDownload the latest release, then run:\n33\t\n34\t```bash\n35\t./domain-check serve\n36\t# Listening on :8080\n37\t```\n38\t\n39\tOpen `http://localhost:8080` in your browser.\n40\t\n41\t### Docker\n42\t\n43\t```bash\n44\tdocker run -p 8080:8080 ronaldraygun/domain-check\n45\t```\n46\t\n47\t### From Source\n48\t\n49\tRequires [Go 1.23+](https://go.dev/dl/).\n50\t\n51\t```bash\n52\tgit clone https://github.com/jedarden/domain-check.git\n53\tcd domain-check\n54\tgo build -o domain-check ./cmd/domain-check\n55\t./domain-check serve\n56\t```\n57\t\n58\t## API Usage\n59\t\n60\tAll API endpoints are prefixed with `/api/v1/`.\n61\t\n62\t### Check a Single Domain\n63\t\n64\t```bash\n65\tcurl -s 'http://localhost:8080/api/v1/check?d=example.com' | jq\n66\t```\n67\t\n68\t```json\n69\t{\n70\t  \"domain\": \"example.com\",\n71\t  \"available\": true,\n72\t  \"tld\": \"com\",\n73\t  \"checked_at\": \"2026-03-29T14:30:00Z\",\n74\t  \"source\": \"rdap\",\n75\t  \"cached\": false,\n76\t  \"duration_ms\": 112\n77\t}\n78\t```\n79\t\n80\tFor registered domains, the response includes registration details:\n81\t\n82\t```json\n83\t{\n84\t  \"domain\": \"google.com\",\n85\t  \"available\": false,\n86\t  \"tld\": \"com\",\n87\t  \"checked_at\": \"2026-03-29T14:30:00Z\",\n88\t  \"source\": \"rdap\",\n89\t  \"cached\": false,\n90\t  \"duration_ms\": 108,\n91\t  \"registration\": {\n92\t    \"registrar\": \"MarkMonitor Inc.\",\n93\t    \"created\": \"1997-09-15T04:00:00Z\",\n94\t    \"expires\": \"2028-09-14T04:00:00Z\",\n95\t    \"nameservers\": [\"ns1.google.com\", \"ns2.google.com\"],\n96\t    \"status\": [\"client delete prohibited\", \"client transfer prohibited\"]\n97\t  }\n98\t}\n99\t```\n100\t\n101\t### Multi-TLD Check\n102\t\n103\tCheck a name across multiple TLDs in one request:\n104\t\n105\t```bash\n106\tcurl -s 'http://localhost:8080/api/v1/check?d=example&tlds=com,org,dev,net,io' | jq\n107\t```\n108\t\n109\t```json\n110\t{\n111\t  \"name\": \"example\",\n112\t  \"total\": 5,\n113\t  \"succeeded\": 5,\n114\t  \"failed\": 0,\n115\t  \"duration\": \"234ms\",\n116\t  \"results\": [\n117\t    { \"domain\": \"example.com\", \"tld\": \"com\", \"result\": { \"available\": false, \"registration\": { \"registrar\": \"...\" } } },\n118\t    { \"domain\": \"example.org\", \"tld\": \"org\", \"result\": { \"available\": false, \"registration\": { \"registrar\": \"...\" } } },\n119\t    { \"domain\": \"example.dev\", \"tld\": \"dev\", \"result\": { \"available\": true } },\n120\t    { \"domain\": \"example.net\", \"tld\": \"net\", \"result\": { \"available\": false, \"registration\": { \"registrar\": \"...\" } } },\n121\t    { \"domain\": \"example.io\", \"tld\": \"io\", \"result\": { \"available\": true } }\n122\t  ]\n123\t}\n124\t```\n125\t\n126\t### Bulk Check\n127\t\n128\tCheck up to 50 domains in a single POST request (max 64 KB body):\n129\t\n130\t```bash\n131\tcurl -s -X POST 'http://localhost:8080/api/v1/bulk' \\\n132\t  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \\\n133\t  -d '{\"domains\":[\"example.com\",\"example.org\",\"example.dev\"]}' | jq\n134\t```\n135\t\n136\t```json\n137\t{\n138\t  \"total\": 3,\n139\t  \"succeeded\": 3,\n140\t  \"failed\": 0,\n141\t  \"duration\": \"342ms\",\n142\t  \"results\": [\n143\t    { \"domain\": \"example.com\", \"result\": { \"available\": false, \"registration\": { \"registrar\": \"...\" } } },\n144\t    { \"domain\": \"example.org\", \"result\": { \"available\": false, \"registration\": { \"registrar\": \"...\" } } },\n145\t    { \"domain\": \"example.dev\", \"result\": { \"available\": true } }\n146\t  ]\n147\t}\n148\t```\n149\t\n150\t### Health Check\n151\t\n152\t```bash\n153\tcurl -s http://localhost:8080/health\n154\t```\n155\t\n156\t```json\n157\t{\"status\":\"ok\"}\n158\t```\n159\t\n160\t### Rate Limits\n161\t\n162\t| Endpoint | Limit |\n163\t|----------|-------|\n164\t| Web UI (`/check`) | 10 req/min per IP |\n165\t| API (`/api/v1/check`) | 60 req/min per IP |\n166\t| Bulk (`/api/v1/bulk`) | 5 req/min per IP |\n167\t\n168\tRate-limited responses return `429 Too Many Requests` with a `Retry-After` header.\n169\t\n170\t## CLI Usage\n171\t\n172\t### Check a Single Domain\n173\t\n174\t```bash\n175\tdomain-check check example.com\n176\t```\n177\t\n178\tCheck across multiple TLDs:\n179\t\n180\t```bash\n181\tdomain-check check example --tlds com,org,dev\n182\t```\n183\t\n184\tJSON output:\n185\t\n186\t```bash\n187\tdomain-check check example.com --format json\n188\t```\n189\t\n190\t### Bulk Check from File\n191\t\n192\tCreate a file with one domain per line:\n193\t\n194\t```\n195\t# domains.txt\n196\texample.com\n197\texample.org\n198\texample.dev\n199\t```\n200\t\n201\tRun the bulk check:\n202\t\n203\t```bash\n204\tdomain-check bulk domains.txt\n205\t```\n206\t\n207\tWith progress indicator and CSV output:\n208\t\n209\t```bash\n210\tdomain-check bulk domains.txt --progress --format csv\n211\t```\n212\t\n213\tAdjust concurrency (default: 20):\n214\t\n215\t```bash\n216\tdomain-check bulk domains.txt --concurrency 30\n217\t```\n218\t\n219\t### CLI Exit Codes\n220\t\n221\t| Code | Meaning |\n222\t|------|---------|\n223\t| 0 | All checked domains are available |\n224\t| 1 | At least one domain is taken |\n225\t| 2 | Error occurred |\n226\t\n227\t## Self-Hosting\n228\t\n229\t### Binary\n230\t\n231\t```bash\n232\t# Download and run\n233\t./domain-check serve --addr :3000\n234\t\n235\t# Run behind a reverse proxy\n236\t./domain-check serve --trust-proxy --addr :8080\n237\t```\n238\t\n239\t### Docker\n240\t\n241\t```bash\n242\t# Build from source\n243\tdocker build -t domain-check .\n244\t\n245\t# Run\n246\tdocker run -p 8080:8080 domain-check\n247\t\n248\t# With environment variables\n249\tdocker run -p 8080:8080 \\\n250\t  -e DOMCHECK_CACHE_SIZE=50000 \\\n251\t  -e DOMCHECK_TRUST_PROXY=true \\\n252\t  domain-check\n253\t```\n254\t\n255\t### Reverse Proxy (Caddy)\n256\t\n257\t```\n258\tdomain-check.example.com {\n259\t    reverse_proxy localhost:8080\n260\t}\n261\t```\n262\t\n263\t### Reverse Proxy (nginx)\n264\t\n265\t```nginx\n266\tserver {\n267\t    listen 443 ssl;\n268\t    server_name domain-check.example.com;\n269\t\n270\t    location / {\n271\t        proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:8080;\n272\t        proxy_set_header Host $host;\n273\t        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;\n274\t        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;\n275\t        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;\n276\t    }\n277\t}\n278\t```\n279\t\n280\t## Configuration\n281\t\n282\tAll settings can be configured via CLI flags, environment variables (prefixed with `DOMCHECK_`), or a YAML config file. Priority: flags > env vars > config file > defaults.\n283\t\n284\t### Config File\n285\t\n286\tCreate a `config.yaml`:\n287\t\n288\t```yaml\n289\taddr: \":3000\"\n290\tcache-size: 50000\n291\tcache-ttl-available: 10m\n292\tcache-ttl-registered: 2h\n293\ttrust-proxy: true\n294\tcors-origins: \"https://example.com\"\n295\tlog-format: text\n296\tlog-level: info\n297\t```\n298\t\n299\tLoad it with `--config config.yaml` or `DOMCHECK_CONFIG=config.yaml`.\n300\t\n301\t### All Parameters\n302\t\n303\t| Flag | Env Variable | Default | Description |\n304\t|------|-------------|---------|-------------|\n305\t| `--addr` | `DOMCHECK_ADDR` | `:8080` | HTTP listen address |\n306\t| `--config` | `DOMCHECK_CONFIG` | — | Path to YAML config file |\n307\t| `--cache-size` | `DOMCHECK_CACHE_SIZE` | `10000` | LRU cache max entries |\n308\t| `--cache-ttl-available` | `DOMCHECK_CACHE_TTL_AVAILABLE` | `5m` | TTL for available domain results |\n309\t| `--cache-ttl-registered` | `DOMCHECK_CACHE_TTL_REGISTERED` | `1h` | TTL for registered domain results |\n310\t| `--bootstrap-refresh` | `DOMCHECK_BOOTSTRAP_REFRESH` | `24h` | IANA RDAP bootstrap refresh interval |\n311\t| `--trust-proxy` | `DOMCHECK_TRUST_PROXY` | `false` | Trust `X-Forwarded-For` for client IP |\n312\t| `--cors-origins` | `DOMCHECK_CORS_ORIGINS` | `*` | Allowed CORS origins (comma-separated) |\n313\t| `--metrics` | `DOMCHECK_METRICS` | `true` | Enable `/metrics` Prometheus endpoint |\n314\t| `--log-format` | `DOMCHECK_LOG_FORMAT` | `json` | Log format: `json` or `text` |\n315\t| `--log-level` | `DOMCHECK_LOG_LEVEL` | `info` | Log level: `debug`, `info`, `warn`, `error` |\n316\t\n317\t## Architecture\n318\t\n319\tDomain Check is a single Go binary with zero runtime dependencies. Templates and static assets are embedded via `go:embed`.\n320\t\n321\t```\n322\tcmd/domain-check/main.go          # Entry point\n323\tinternal/\n324\t  config/      # Layered config: flags → env → YAML → defaults\n325\t  domain/      # Input validation, IDN, TLD extraction via publicsuffix\n326\t  bootstrap/   # IANA RDAP bootstrap loader with 24h cache refresh\n327\t  rdap/        # RDAP client, response parser, per-registry rate limiting\n328\t  whois/       # WHOIS fallback for ccTLDs without RDAP\n329\t  cache/       # In-memory bounded LRU with per-status TTLs\n330\t  httpclient/  # SSRF-safe HTTP client (private IP blocking)\n331\t  ratelimit/   # Per-IP rate limiter middleware\n332\t  server/      # HTTP server, router, middleware, API handlers\n333\t  web/         # HTML templates, static assets, template handlers\n334\t  cli/         # CLI subcommands (check, bulk)\n335\tweb/\n336\t  templates/   # HTML templates (layout, index, result)\n337\t  static/      # CSS, JS, favicon (embedded)\n338\t```\n339\t\n340\t### Request Flow\n341\t\n342\t1. **Validate** — normalize input, convert IDN to punycode, extract TLD via publicsuffix\n343\t2. **Cache check** — return cached result if fresh\n344\t3. **Rate limit** — enforce per-registry limits (Verisign 10/s, Google 1/s)\n345\t4. **RDAP query** — query the authoritative registry server over HTTPS\n346\t5. **Parse** — extract availability, registration details, nameservers\n347\t6. **Cache store** — cache result with per-status TTL (5min available, 1h registered)\n348\t7. **Respond** — return structured JSON\n349\t\n350\t### Caching\n351\t\n352\t| Result Type | TTL | Rationale |\n353\t|-------------|-----|-----------|\n354\t| Available | 5 min | Availability can change quickly |\n355\t| Registered | 1 hour | Registered domains rarely become available |\n356\t| Error | 30 sec | Retry soon on transient failures |\n357\t\n358\t### Security\n359\t\n360\t- **SSRF protection** — private IP blocking, URL allowlist, redirect validation\n361\t- **Security headers** — CSP, X-Frame-Options, X-Content-Type-Options, Referrer-Policy\n362\t- **Non-root Docker** — runs as `appuser` (UID 1000)\n363\t- **No tracking** — no analytics, no cookies, no data retention\n364\t\n365\t## Development\n366\t\n367\t```bash\n368\t# Build\n369\tgo build -o domain-check ./cmd/domain-check\n370\t\n371\t# Run tests\n372\tgo test ./...\n373\t\n374\t# Run tests with verbose output\n375\tgo test -v ./internal/domain/\n376\t\n377\t# Fuzz testing\n378\tgo test -fuzz=. -fuzztime=30s ./internal/domain/\n379\t\n380\t# Lint\n381\tgolangci-lint run\n382\t```\n383\t\n384\t## Documentation\n385\t\n386\t- [docs/research/](docs/research/) — Research on domain checking methods, RDAP protocol, rate limits, accuracy testing\n387\t- [docs/plan/plan.md](docs/plan/plan.md) — Complete architecture plan\n388\t\n389\t## Contributing\n390\t\n391\t1. RDAP was 100% accurate across all test cases. See [docs/research/05-accuracy-comparison.md](docs/research/05-accuracy-comparison.md).\n\n## Quick Start\n\n### Binary (Linux/macOS)\n\nDownload the latest release, then run:\n\n```bash\n./domain-check serve\n# Listening on :8080\n```\n\nOpen `http://localhost:8080` in your browser.\n\n### Docker\n\n```bash\ndocker run -p 8080:8080 ronaldraygun/domain-check\n```\n\n### From Source\n\nRequires [Go 1.23+](https://go.dev/dl/).\n\n```bash\ngit clone https://github.com/jedarden/domain-check.git\ncd domain-check\ngo build -o domain-check ./cmd/domain-check\n./domain-check serve\n```\n\n## API Usage\n\nAll API endpoints are prefixed with `/api/v1/`.\n\n### Check a Single Domain\n\n```bash\ncurl -s 'http://localhost:8080/api/v1/check?d=example.com' | jq\n```\n\n```json\n{\n  \"domain\": \"example.com\",\n  \"available\": true,\n  \"tld\": \"com\",\n  \"checked_at\": \"2026-03-29T14:30:00Z\",\n  \"source\": \"rdap\",\n  \"cached\": false,\n  \"duration_ms\": 112\n}\n```\n\nFor registered domains, the response includes registration details:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"domain\": \"google.com\",\n  \"available\": false,\n  \"tld\": \"com\",\n  \"checked_at\": \"2026-03-29T14:30:00Z\",\n  \"source\": \"rdap\",\n  \"cached\": false,\n  \"duration_ms\": 108,\n  \"registration\": {\n    \"registrar\": \"MarkMonitor Inc.\",\n    \"created\": \"1997-09-15T04:00:00Z\",\n    \"expires\": \"2028-09-14T04:00:00Z\",\n    \"nameservers\": [\"ns1.google.com\", \"ns2.google.com\"],\n    \"status\": [\"client delete prohibited\", \"client transfer prohibited\"]\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### Multi-TLD Check\n\nCheck a name across multiple TLDs in one request:\n\n```bash\ncurl -s 'http://localhost:8080/api/v1/check?d=example&tlds=com,org,dev,net,io' | jq\n```\n\n```json\n{\n  \"name\": \"example\",\n  \"total\": 5,\n  \"succeeded\": 5,\n  \"failed\": 0,\n  \"duration\": \"234ms\",\n  \"results\": [\n    { \"domain\": \"example.com\", \"tld\": \"com\", \"result\": { \"available\": false, \"registration\": { \"registrar\": \"...\" } } },\n    { \"domain\": \"example.org\", \"tld\": \"org\", \"result\": { \"available\": false, \"registration\": { \"registrar\": \"...\" } } },\n    { \"domain\": \"example.dev\", \"tld\": \"dev\", \"result\": { \"available\": true } },\n    { \"domain\": \"example.net\", \"tld\": \"net\", \"result\": { \"available\": false, \"registration\": { \"registrar\": \"...\" } } },\n    { \"domain\": \"example.io\", \"tld\": \"io\", \"result\": { \"available\": true } }\n  ]\n}\n```\n\n### Bulk Check\n\nCheck up to 50 domains in a single POST request (max 64 KB body):\n\n```bash\ncurl -s -X POST 'http://localhost:8080/api/v1/bulk' \\\n  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \\\n  -d '{\"domains\":[\"example.com\",\"example.org\",\"example.dev\"]}' | jq\n```\n\n```json\n{\n  \"total\": 3,\n  \"succeeded\": 3,\n  \"failed\": 0,\n  \"duration\": \"342ms\",\n  \"results\": [\n    { \"domain\": \"example.com\", \"result\": { \"available\": false, \"registration\": { \"registrar\": \"...\" } } },\n    { \"domain\": \"example.org\", \"result\": { \"available\": false, \"registration\": { \"registrar\": \"...\" } } },\n    { \"domain\": \"example.dev\", \"result\": { \"available\": true } }\n  ]\n}\n```\n\n### Health Check\n\n```bash\ncurl -s http://localhost:8080/health\n```\n\n```json\n{\"status\":\"ok\"}\n```\n\n### Rate Limits\n\n| Endpoint | Limit |\n|----------|-------|\n| Web UI (`/check`) | 10 req/min per IP |\n| API (`/api/v1/check`) | 60 req/min per IP |\n| Bulk (`/api/v1/bulk`) | 5 req/min per IP |\n\nRate-limited responses return `429 Too Many Requests` with a `Retry-After` header.\n\n## CLI Usage\n\n### Check a Single Domain\n\n```bash\ndomain-check check example.com\n```\n\nCheck across multiple TLDs:\n\n```bash\ndomain-check check example --tlds com,org,dev\n```\n\nJSON output:\n\n```bash\ndomain-check check example.com --format json\n```\n\n### Bulk Check from File\n\nCreate a file with one domain per line:\n\n```\n# domains.txt\nexample.com\nexample.org\nexample.dev\n```\n\nRun the bulk check:\n\n```bash\ndomain-check bulk domains.txt\n```\n\nWith progress indicator and CSV output:\n\n```bash\ndomain-check bulk domains.txt --progress --format csv\n```\n\nAdjust concurrency (default: 20):\n\n```bash\ndomain-check bulk domains.txt --concurrency 30\n```\n\n### CLI Exit Codes\n\n| Code | Meaning |\n|------|---------|\n| 0 | All checked domains are available |\n| 1 | At least one domain is taken |\n| 2 | Error occurred |\n\n## Self-Hosting\n\n### Binary\n\n```bash\n# Download and run\n./domain-check serve --addr :3000\n\n# Run behind a reverse proxy\n./domain-check serve --trust-proxy --addr :8080\n```\n\n### Docker\n\n```bash\n# Build from source\ndocker build -t domain-check .\n\n# Run\ndocker run -p 8080:8080 domain-check\n\n# With environment variables\ndocker run -p 8080:8080 \\\n  -e DOMCHECK_CACHE_SIZE=50000 \\\n  -e DOMCHECK_TRUST_PROXY=true \\\n  domain-check\n```\n\n### Reverse Proxy (Caddy)\n\n```\ndomain-check.example.com {\n    reverse_proxy localhost:8080\n}\n```\n\n### Reverse Proxy (nginx)\n\n```nginx\nserver {\n    listen 443 ssl;\n    server_name domain-check.example.com;\n\n    location / {\n        proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:8080;\n        proxy_set_header Host $host;\n        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;\n        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;\n        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;\n    }\n}\n```\n\n## Configuration\n\nAll settings can be configured via CLI flags, environment variables (prefixed with `DOMCHECK_`), or a YAML config file. Priority: flags > env vars > config file > defaults.\n\n### Config File\n\nCreate a `config.yaml`:\n\n```yaml\naddr: \":3000\"\ncache-size: 50000\ncache-ttl-available: 10m\ncache-ttl-registered: 2h\ntrust-proxy: true\ncors-origins: \"https://example.com\"\nlog-format: text\nlog-level: info\n```\n\nLoad it with `--config config.yaml` or `DOMCHECK_CONFIG=config.yaml`.\n\n### All Parameters\n\n| Flag | Env Variable | Default | Description |\n|------|-------------|---------|-------------|\n| `--addr` | `DOMCHECK_ADDR` | `:8080` | HTTP listen address |\n| `--config` | `DOMCHECK_CONFIG` | — | Path to YAML config file |\n| `--cache-size` | `DOMCHECK_CACHE_SIZE` | `10000` | LRU cache max entries |\n| `--cache-ttl-available` | `DOMCHECK_CACHE_TTL_AVAILABLE` | `5m` | TTL for available domain results |\n| `--cache-ttl-registered` | `DOMCHECK_CACHE_TTL_REGISTERED` | `1h` | TTL for registered domain results |\n| `--bootstrap-refresh` | `DOMCHECK_BOOTSTRAP_REFRESH` | `24h` | IANA RDAP bootstrap refresh interval |\n| `--trust-proxy` | `DOMCHECK_TRUST_PROXY` | `false` | Trust `X-Forwarded-For` for client IP |\n| `--cors-origins` | `DOMCHECK_CORS_ORIGINS` | `*` | Allowed CORS origins (comma-separated) |\n| `--metrics` | `DOMCHECK_METRICS` | `true` | Enable `/metrics` Prometheus endpoint |\n| `--log-format` | `DOMCHECK_LOG_FORMAT` | `json` | Log format: `json` or `text` |\n| `--log-level` | `DOMCHECK_LOG_LEVEL` | `info` | Log level: `debug`, `info`, `warn`, `error` |\n\n## Architecture\n\nDomain Check is a single Go binary with zero runtime dependencies. Templates and static assets are embedded via `go:embed`.\n\n```\ncmd/domain-check/main.go          # Entry point\ninternal/\n  config/      # Layered config: flags → env → YAML → defaults\n  domain/      # Input validation, IDN, TLD extraction via publicsuffix\n  bootstrap/   # IANA RDAP bootstrap loader with 24h cache refresh\n  rdap/        # RDAP client, response parser, per-registry rate limiting\n  whois/       # WHOIS fallback for ccTLDs without RDAP\n  cache/       # In-memory bounded LRU with per-status TTLs\n  httpclient/  # SSRF-safe HTTP client (private IP blocking)\n  ratelimit/   # Per-IP rate limiter middleware\n  server/      # HTTP server, router, middleware, API handlers\n  web/         # HTML templates, static assets, template handlers\n  cli/         # CLI subcommands (check, bulk)\nweb/\n  templates/   # HTML templates (layout, index, result)\n  static/      # CSS, JS, favicon (embedded)\n```\n\n### Request Flow\n\n1. **Validate** — normalize input, convert IDN to punycode, extract TLD via publicsuffix\n2. **Cache check** — return cached result if fresh\n3. **Rate limit** — enforce per-registry limits (Verisign 10/s, Google 1/s)\n4. **RDAP query** — query the authoritative registry server over HTTPS\n5. **Parse** — extract availability, registration details, nameservers\n6. **Cache store** — cache result with per-status TTL (5min available, 1h registered)\n7. **Respond** — return structured JSON\n\n### Caching\n\n| Result Type | TTL | Rationale |\n|-------------|-----|-----------|\n| Available | 5 min | Availability can change quickly |\n| Registered | 1 hour | Registered domains rarely become available |\n| Error | 30 sec | Retry soon on transient failures |\n\n### Security\n\n- **SSRF protection** — private IP blocking, URL allowlist, redirect validation\n- **Security headers** — CSP, X-Frame-Options, X-Content-Type-Options, Referrer-Policy\n- **Non-root Docker** — runs as `appuser` (UID 1000)\n- **No tracking** — no analytics, no cookies, no data retention\n\n## Development\n\n```bash\n# Build\ngo build -o domain-check ./cmd/domain-check\n\n# Run tests\ngo test ./...\n\n# Run tests with verbose output\ngo test -v ./internal/domain/\n\n# Fuzz testing\ngo test -fuzz=. -fuzztime=30s ./internal/domain/\n\n# Lint\ngolangci-lint run\n```\n\n## Documentation\n\n- [docs/research/](docs/research/) — Research on domain checking methods, RDAP protocol, rate limits, accuracy testing\n- [docs/plan/plan.md](docs/plan/plan.md) — Complete architecture plan\n\n## Contributing\n\n1.
